RE 104537Z — MEASURE. Disabling single-thread pruning throughout bare-king searches made actual mating worse. At 250,000 nodes, stock mated on 48 of the 96 generated hard starts. The full no-pruning switch mated on 32. There were 19 stock-only and three full-only successes, for a paired difference of −16.67 percentage points [−26.04,−8.33]. With bishop and knight stock mated on 3/48 versus 1/48 full. With opposite-colored bishops stock mated on 45/48 versus 31/48 full. At 20k it was 13/96 versus 3/96, difference −10.42 points [−16.67,−5.21]. The queen and rook controls each mated on 16/16 for both builds and both budgets. Keep the shipped single-thread reduction cap. The full switch missed the prespecified +5-point bar and both minor material strata declined.

The distinction between a theoretically winning move and delivering checkmate mattered on this panel. At 250k stock drew 47 by the fifty-move rule and one by threefold. Full drew 64 by the fifty-move rule. No attacker in any of the 512 games ever played a move that dropped its unclocked table W/D/L. Every non-mate was still an unclocked table win at the moment the rule drew the game. These positions had a zero initial halfmove counter and were generated uniformly as declared, so 48/96 is not a natural game conversion estimate. The existing independent 8,192-game study (105833Z) keeps real histories and starting clocks and tests a separate root move policy; its results are outstanding. Neon, please independently recount the paired synthetic result, especially KBN 3/48 versus 1/48 and whether the default switch should stay.

Reproduce using source/src/search.cpp:46–64,2052–2071,2406–2425, raw compiler commands/hashes below, full r67 loop options then cold reset once per game. The full 104537Z protocol fixes position generation, the table defender, remaining history and node budgets. All five pins passed twice; all 512 games were legally replayed and every defender reply independently selected from all legal children. Thirty-two complete fresh-process sentinels reproduced all moves and node sums. The negative-control FEN used in the final control was 8/8/8/8/8/1K6/1B6/kN6 b - - 0 1, where the only drawing capture is a1b1; the earlier candidate fixture also had a noncapture draw and was replaced before the game results. Positive mate g6g7 was verified. For each source position calculate full mate indicator minus stock, then take 10,000 whole-position paired resamples with PCG64(202611231045), same resampled indices for both versions. Here are the raw moves, FENs, material, actual ending and first theoretical loss for every cell.
